   395: function pickPrizeHour(prize) {
   396:   const candidates = [
   397:     prize?.closeHour,
   398:     prize?.close_hour,
   399:     prize?.hour,
   400:     prize?.horario,
   401:     prize?.drawHour,
   402:     prize?.draw_hour,
   403:     prize?.lotteryCloseHour,
   404:   ];
   405:   for (const c of candidates) {
   406:     const n = normalizeHourLike(c);
   407:     if (n) return n;
   408:   }
   409:   const textCandidates = [prize?.lotteryLabel, prize?.loteriaLabel, prize?.label, prize?.name, prize?.title];
   410:   for (const t of textCandidates) {
   411:     const ex = extractHourFromText(t);
   412:     if (ex) return ex;
   413:   }
   414:   return "";
   415: }

   419: function digitFromKey(key) {
   420:   const s = String(key || "");
   421:   let h = 0;
   422:   for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i += 1) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;

   395: function pickPrizeHour(prize) {
   396:   const candidates = [
   397:     prize?.closeHour,
   398:     prize?.close_hour,
   399:     prize?.hour,
   400:     prize?.horario,
   401:     prize?.drawHour,
   402:     prize?.draw_hour,
   403:     prize?.lotteryCloseHour,
   404:   ];
   405:   for (const c of candidates) {
   406:     const n = normalizeHourLike(c);
   407:     if (n) return n;
   408:   }
   409:   const textCandidates = [prize?.lotteryLabel, prize?.loteriaLabel, prize?.label, prize?.name, prize?.title];
   410:   for (const t of textCandidates) {
   411:     const ex = extractHourFromText(t);
   412:     if (ex) return ex;
   413:   }
   414:   return "";
   415: }
   416: 
   417: /* ========= milhar palpite ========= */
   418: 
   419: function digitFromKey(key) {
   420:   const s = String(key || "");
   421:   let h = 0;
   422:   for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i += 1) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;
   423:   return String(h % 10);
   424: }

    89: function isTodos(v) {
    90:   return String(v || "").trim().toLowerCase() === "todos";
    91: }

    93: /**
    94:  * Normaliza "hora" em formato HH:MM (quando possível)
    95:  */
    96: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
    97:   const s0 = String(value ?? "").trim();
    98:   if (!s0) return "";
    99:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   100: 
   101:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   102:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
   103: 
   104:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
   105:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
   106: 
   107:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   108:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
   109: 
   110:   return s0.trim();
   111: }
   112: 
   113: /**
   114:  * ✅ Bucketiza para HH:00, ignorando minutos/segundos.
   115:  * Ex: "21:10" -> "21:00", "21h" -> "21:00", "09:00" -> "09:00"
   116:  */
   117: function toHourBucketHH00(value) {
   118:   const norm = normalizeHourLike(value);
   119:   if (!norm) return "";
   120:   const m = String(norm).match(/^(\d{2})/);
   121:   if (!m) return "";

   110:   return s0.trim();
   111: }
   112: 
   113: /**
   114:  * ✅ Bucketiza para HH:00, ignorando minutos/segundos.
   115:  * Ex: "21:10" -> "21:00", "21h" -> "21:00", "09:00" -> "09:00"
   116:  */
   117: function toHourBucketHH00(value) {
   118:   const norm = normalizeHourLike(value);
   119:   if (!norm) return "";
   120:   const m = String(norm).match(/^(\d{2})/);
   121:   if (!m) return "";
   122:   const hh = m[1];
   123:   const n = Number(hh);
   124:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 23) return "";
   125:   return `${hh}:00`;
   126: }
   127: 
   128: function extractHourFromText(text) {
   129:   const s = String(text ?? "").trim();
   130:   if (!s) return "";
   131:   const mh = s.match(/\b(\d{1,2})\s*(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)\b/i);
   132:   if (mh) return `${pad2(mh[1])}:00`;
   133:   const mIso = s.match(/\b(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::\d{2})?\b/);
   134:   if (mIso) return `${pad2(mIso[1])}:${pad2(mIso[2])}`;
   135:   const mBare = s.match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{1,2})(?:\D|$)/);
   136:   if (mBare) {
   137:     const n = Number(mBare[1]);
   138:     if (Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && n <= 23) return `${pad2(n)}:00`;
   139:   }
   140:   return "";
   141: }

   419: function digitFromKey(key) {
   420:   const s = String(key || "");
   421:   let h = 0;
   422:   for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i += 1) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;
   423:   return String(h % 10);
   424: }

   113: /**
   114:  * ✅ Bucketiza para HH:00, ignorando minutos/segundos.
   115:  * Ex: "21:10" -> "21:00", "21h" -> "21:00", "09:00" -> "09:00"
   116:  */
   117: function toHourBucketHH00(value) {
   118:   const norm = normalizeHourLike(value);
   119:   if (!norm) return "";
   120:   const m = String(norm).match(/^(\d{2})/);
   121:   if (!m) return "";
   122:   const hh = m[1];
   123:   const n = Number(hh);
   124:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 23) return "";
   125:   return `${hh}:00`;
   126: }
   127: 
   128: function extractHourFromText(text) {
   129:   const s = String(text ?? "").trim();
   130:   if (!s) return "";
   131:   const mh = s.match(/\b(\d{1,2})\s*(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)\b/i);
   132:   if (mh) return `${pad2(mh[1])}:00`;
   133:   const mIso = s.match(/\b(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::\d{2})?\b/);
   134:   if (mIso) return `${pad2(mIso[1])}:${pad2(mIso[2])}`;
   135:   const mBare = s.match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{1,2})(?:\D|$)/);
   136:   if (mBare) {
   137:     const n = Number(mBare[1]);
   138:     if (Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && n <= 23) return `${pad2(n)}:00`;
   139:   }
   140:   return "";
   141: }

   947:         const got = toHourBucketHH00(hr);
   948:         if (!got || got !== want) return false;
   949:       }
   950:

   203: function getWeekdayPTBRFromYMD(ymd) {
   204:   const dt = ymdToUTCDate(ymd);
   205:   if (!dt) return "";
   206:   const day = dt.getUTCDay();
   207:   const map = ["Domingo", "Segunda-Feira", "Terça-Feira", "Quarta-Feira", "Quinta-Feira", "Sexta-Feira", "Sábado"];
   208:   return map[day] || "";
   209: }
   210: function monthNamePTBR(m) {
   211:   const map = [
   212:     "Janeiro",
   213:     "Fevereiro",
   214:     "Março",
   215:     "Abril",
   216:     "Maio",
   217:     "Junho",
   218:     "Julho",
   219:     "Agosto",
   220:     "Setembro",
   221:     "Outubro",
   222:     "Novembro",
   223:     "Dezembro",
   224:   ];
   225:   return map[(Number(m) || 0) - 1] || "";
   226: }

   228: /* ========= centenas por grupo ========= */
   229: 
   230: function dezenasDoGrupo(grupo) {
   231:   const g = Number(grupo);
   232:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
   233: 
   234:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
   235: 
   236:   return [start, start + 1, start + 2, start + 3].map((n) => {
   237:     const dezena = n % 100;
   238:     return pad2(dezena);
   239:   });
   240: }
   241: function centenas40DoGrupo(grupo) {
   242:   const dezenas = dezenasDoGrupo(grupo);
   243:   const out = [];
   244:   for (const dz of dezenas) {
   245:     for (let p = 0; p <= 9; p += 1) out.push(`${p}${dz}`);
   246:   }
   247:   return out;
   248: }

    14:  * Centenas+ (PREMIUM)

    16:  * - chunks em paralelo (concorrência controlada)
    17:  * - cache em memória por (lottery + closeHour + positions)

    19:  * ✅ UX:
    20:  * - persistência de filtros no localStorage (não apaga ao trocar de página)
    21:  * - status de progresso por chunks
    22:  *
    23:  * ✅ Imagem nítida:
    24:  * - tenta usar getImgFromGrupo(grupo, 128) quando disponível
    25:  * - tenta "upar" _64/_96 -> _128 no banner e nos cards

    27:  * ✅ FIX (Horário):
    28:  * - Firestore/King pode salvar close_hour como 21:10 / 11:10 etc.

   342:   return out;

   344: 
   345: function normalizeDrawsResult(maybe) {
   346:   if (Array.isArray(maybe)) return maybe;
   347:   if (Array.isArray(maybe?.draws)) return maybe.draws;
   348:   if (Array.isArray(maybe?.drawsRaw)) return maybe.drawsRaw;
   349:   if (Array.isArray(maybe?.data)) return maybe.data;
   350:   if (Array.isArray(maybe?.results)) return maybe.results;
   351:   return [];
   352: }
   353: 
   354: /**
   355:  * ✅ Pega prizes flat vindas do service em qualquer chave comum
   356:  */
   357: function normalizePrizesArray(res) {
   358:   const candidates = [
   359:     res?.prizesAllSorted,
   360:     res?.prizesAll,
   361:     res?.prizes,
   362:     res?.prizeRows,
   363:     res?.items,
   364:     res?.data?.prizesAllSorted,
   365:     res?.data?.prizesAll,
   366:     res?.data?.prizes,
   367:   ];
   368:   for (const c of candidates) {
   369:     if (Array.isArray(c)) return c;
   370:   }
   371:   return [];
   372: }
   373: 
   374: /**
   375:  * ✅ Extrai ymd/hora direto da prize (para quando draws vierem sem prizes)

   424: }
   425: 
   426: 
   427: /* ========= utils: concorrência ========= */
   428: 
   429: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
   430:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
   431:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 3);
   432:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
   433:   let idx = 0;
   434: 
   435:   async function worker() {
   436:     while (idx < arr.length) {
   437:       const current = idx++;
   438:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
   439:     }
   440:   }
   441: 
   442:   await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, worker));
   443:   return results;
   444: }
   445: 
   446: /* ========= imagem: tentar _128 ========= */
   447: 
   448: function upgradeImg(src, target = 128) {
   449:   const s = String(src || "").trim();
   450:   if (!s) return "";
   451:   return s.replace(/_(64|96|128)\.(png|jpg|jpeg|webp)$/i, `_${target}.$2`);
   452: }
   453: 
   454: function tryGetImg(getImgFromGrupo, grupo, size) {
   455:   try {
   456:     const v = getImgFromGrupo?.(grupo, size);
   457:     if (v) return String(v);

   264: function resolveHourFilter({ closeHour = null, closeHourBucket = null }) {
   265:   const bucket = toHourBucket(closeHourBucket);
   266:   if (bucket) return { kind: "bucket", bucket, hhmm: null };
   267: 
   268:   const hhmm = closeHour ? normalizeHourLike(closeHour) : null;
   269:   if (hhmm) return { kind: "exact", bucket: null, hhmm };
   270: 
   271:   return { kind: null, bucket: null, hhmm: null };
   272: }

  1029: function federalHourPriority(draw) {
  1030:   const h = normalizeHourLike(
  1031:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""

  1041:     return 100000 + n;
  1042:   }
  1043: 
  1044:   if (h === "19:00") return 200000 + n;
  1045:   if (h === "20:00") return 190000 + n;
  1046:   return 100000 + n;

######## PADRÃO: normalizeHour ########
   192: 
   193: /* =========================
   194:    Utils
   195: ========================= */
   196: 
   197: /**
   198:  * ✅ Normaliza "horário" para HH:MM
   199:  */
   200: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
   201:   const s0 = String(value ?? "").trim();
   202:   if (!s0) return "";
   203: 
   204:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   205: 
   206:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   207:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
   208: 
   209:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
   210:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
   211: 
   212:   const mHm = s.match(/^(\d{3,4})$/);
   213:   if (mHm) {
   214:     const hh = String(mHm[1]).slice(0, -2);
   215:     const mm = String(mHm[1]).slice(-2);
   216:     if (/^\d{1,2}$/.test(hh) && /^\d{2}$/.test(mm)) {
   217:       return `${pad2(hh)}:${mm}`;
   218:     }
   219:   }
   220: 
   221:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   222:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
   223: 
   224:   return s0.trim();
   225: }

   260:    - exact  SOMENTE via closeHour
   261:    (elimina ambiguidade e "filtro fantasma")
   262: ========================= */
   263: 
   264: function resolveHourFilter({ closeHour = null, closeHourBucket = null }) {
   265:   const bucket = toHourBucket(closeHourBucket);
   266:   if (bucket) return { kind: "bucket", bucket, hhmm: null };
   267: 
   268:   const hhmm = closeHour ? normalizeHourLike(closeHour) : null;
   269:   if (hhmm) return { kind: "exact", bucket: null, hhmm };
   270: 
   271:   return { kind: null, bucket: null, hhmm: null };
   272: }
   273: 
   274: function drawPassesHourFilter(draw, hourFilter) {
   275:   if (!hourFilter || !hourFilter.kind) return true;

  1042:   }

  1616: function hourToNumSafe(hhmm) {
  1617:   const s = normalizeHourLike(hhmm);

  1619:   if (!m) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  1620:   return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);

######## PADRÃO: normalizeHourLike ########
   192: 
   193: /* =========================
   194:    Utils
   195: ========================= */
   196: 
   197: /**
   198:  * ✅ Normaliza "horário" para HH:MM
   199:  */
   200: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
   201:   const s0 = String(value ?? "").trim();
   202:   if (!s0) return "";
   203: 
   204:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   205: 
   206:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   207:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
   208: 
   209:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
   210:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
   211: 
   212:   const mHm = s.match(/^(\d{3,4})$/);
   213:   if (mHm) {
   214:     const hh = String(mHm[1]).slice(0, -2);
   215:     const mm = String(mHm[1]).slice(-2);
   216:     if (/^\d{1,2}$/.test(hh) && /^\d{2}$/.test(mm)) {
   217:       return `${pad2(hh)}:${mm}`;
   218:     }
   219:   }
   220: 
   221:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   222:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
   223: 
   224:   return s0.trim();
   225: }

   260:    - exact  SOMENTE via closeHour
   261:    (elimina ambiguidade e "filtro fantasma")
   262: ========================= */

   264: function resolveHourFilter({ closeHour = null, closeHourBucket = null }) {
   265:   const bucket = toHourBucket(closeHourBucket);
   266:   if (bucket) return { kind: "bucket", bucket, hhmm: null };
   267: 
   268:   const hhmm = closeHour ? normalizeHourLike(closeHour) : null;
   269:   if (hhmm) return { kind: "exact", bucket: null, hhmm };
   270: 
   271:   return { kind: null, bucket: null, hhmm: null };
   272: }

  1616: function hourToNumSafe(hhmm) {
  1617:   const s = normalizeHourLike(hhmm);
  1618:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
  1619:   if (!m) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  1620:   return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);
  1621: }

######## PADRÃO: toHourBucket ########
   227: function mhxToInt(v) {
   228:   const n = Number(v);
   229:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
   230: }

   232: /**
   233:  * ✅ Bucket "09h", "11h"…
   234:  */
   235: function toHourBucket(value) {
   236:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
   237:   if (!s || s.toLowerCase() === "todos") return null;
   238: 
   239:   const mh = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
   240:   if (mh) return `${pad2(mhxToInt(mh[1]))}h`;
   241: 
   242:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);
   243:   if (m1) return `${pad2(mhxToInt(m1[1]))}h`;
   244: 
   245:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   246:   if (m2) return `${pad2(mhxToInt(m2[1]))}h`;
   247: 
   248:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
   249:   if (m3) return `${pad2(mhxToInt(m3[1]))}h`;
   250: 
   251:   const m4 = s.match(/^(\d{3,4})$/);
   252:   if (m4) return `${pad2(mhxToInt(String(m4[1]).slice(0, -2)))}h`;
   253: 
   254:   return null;
   255: }

   258:    ✅ FIX DEFINITIVO: filtro de horário (bucket vs exato)
   259:    - bucket SOMENTE via closeHourBucket
   260:    - exact  SOMENTE via closeHour

   257: /* =========================

   259:    - bucket SOMENTE via closeHourBucket

   261:    (elimina ambiguidade e "filtro fantasma")

   264: function resolveHourFilter({ closeHour = null, closeHourBucket = null }) {
   265:   const bucket = toHourBucket(closeHourBucket);
   266:   if (bucket) return { kind: "bucket", bucket, hhmm: null };
   267: 
   268:   const hhmm = closeHour ? normalizeHourLike(closeHour) : null;
   269:   if (hhmm) return { kind: "exact", bucket: null, hhmm };
   270: 
   271:   return { kind: null, bucket: null, hhmm: null };
   272: }
   273: 
   274: function drawPassesHourFilter(draw, hourFilter) {
   275:   if (!hourFilter || !hourFilter.kind) return true;
   276: 
   277:   const raw =
   278:     draw?.close_hour ?? draw?.closeHour ?? draw?.hour ?? draw?.hora ?? "";
   279:   const norm = normalizeHourLike(raw);
   280: 
   281:   if (hourFilter.kind === "exact") {
   282:     return norm === normalizeHourLike(hourFilter.hhmm);
   283:   }
   284: 
   285:   if (hourFilter.kind === "bucket") {
   286:     return toHourBucket(norm) === toHourBucket(hourFilter.bucket);
   287:   }
   288: 
   289:   return true;
   290: }

   278:     draw?.close_hour ?? draw?.closeHour ?? draw?.hour ?? draw?.hora ?? "";
   279:   const norm = normalizeHourLike(raw);
   280: 
   281:   if (hourFilter.kind === "exact") {
   282:     return norm === normalizeHourLike(hourFilter.hhmm);
   283:   }
   284: 
   285:   if (hourFilter.kind === "bucket") {
   286:     return toHourBucket(norm) === toHourBucket(hourFilter.bucket);
   287:   }
   288: 
   289:   return true;
   290: }

   442: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
   443:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
   444:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
   445:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
   446:   let idx = 0;
   447: 
   448:   async function worker() {
   449:     while (true) {
   450:       const current = idx;
   451:       idx += 1;
   452:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
   453:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
   454:     }
   455:   }
   456: 
   457:   await Promise.all(
   458:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
   459:   );
   460:   return results;
   461: }

  1565: function normalizeLotteriesList(lotteries) {
  1566:   const arr = Array.isArray(lotteries) ? lotteries : [];
  1567:   const cleaned = arr
  1568:     .map((x) => String(x ?? "").trim().toUpperCase())
  1569:     .filter(Boolean);
  1570:   return cleaned.length ? Array.from(new Set(cleaned)) : null;
  1571: }

  1573: function drawPassesLotteriesFilter(draw, lotteriesArr) {
  1574:   if (!lotteriesArr || !lotteriesArr.length) return true;
  1575: 
  1576:   const identities = [
  1577:     draw?.lottery_key,
  1578:     draw?.lotteryKey,
  1579:     draw?.lottery_code,
  1580:     draw?.lotteryCode,
  1581:     draw?.lot_code,
  1582:     draw?.lotCode,
  1583:     draw?.lot,
  1584:     draw?.code,
  1585:   ]
  1586:     .map((value) => String(value ?? "").trim().toUpperCase())
  1587:     .filter(Boolean);
  1588: 
  1589:   return identities.some((identity) =>
  1590:     lotteriesArr.includes(identity)
  1591:   );
  1592: }

   441: 
   442: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
   443:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
   444:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
   445:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
   446:   let idx = 0;
   447: 
   448:   async function worker() {
   449:     while (true) {
   450:       const current = idx;
   451:       idx += 1;
   452:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
   453:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
   454:     }
   455:   }
   456: 
   457:   await Promise.all(
   458:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
   459:   );
   460:   return results;
   461: }

  1007: function buildPrizeSignature(draw) {
  1008:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
  1009:   if (!prizes.length) return "";
  1010: 
  1011:   const parts = prizes
  1012:     .map((p) => {
  1013:       const pos = Number(p?.position);
  1014:       const grupo = Number(p?.grupo);
  1015:       const numero = toPrizeDigitsByPosition(
  1016:         p?.numero ?? p?.milhar ?? p?.number ?? p?.num ?? p?.valor ?? "",
  1017:         pos
  1018:       );
  1019: 
  1020:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || !Number.isFinite(grupo) || !numero) return "";
  1021:       return `${pad2(pos)}:${pad2(grupo)}:${numero}`;
  1022:     })
  1023:     .filter(Boolean)
  1024:     .sort();
  1025: 
  1026:   return parts.join("|");
  1027: }

   442: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
   443:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
   444:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
   445:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
   446:   let idx = 0;
   447: 
   448:   async function worker() {
   449:     while (true) {
   450:       const current = idx;
   451:       idx += 1;
   452:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
   453:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
   454:     }
   455:   }
   456: 
   457:   await Promise.all(
   458:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
   459:   );
   460:   return results;
   461: }
   462:

  1565: function normalizeLotteriesList(lotteries) {
  1566:   const arr = Array.isArray(lotteries) ? lotteries : [];
  1567:   const cleaned = arr
  1568:     .map((x) => String(x ?? "").trim().toUpperCase())
  1569:     .filter(Boolean);
  1570:   return cleaned.length ? Array.from(new Set(cleaned)) : null;
  1571: }

  1573: function drawPassesLotteriesFilter(draw, lotteriesArr) {

  1575:

  1577:     draw?.lottery_key,

  1581:     draw?.lot_code,
  1582:     draw?.lotCode,
  1583:     draw?.lot,

  1681:     return outEmpty;

  1684:   while (cursorTo >= fromYmd && lastSeen.size < 25) {

  1695:       mode: "detailed",
  1696:     });

  1699:

  1701:       if (!d) continue;

   321: function sortDrawsLocal(draws) {
   322:   const toNum = (h) => {
   323:     const s = normalizeHourLike(h);
   324:     const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
   325:     if (!m) return 0;
   326:     return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);
   327:   };
   328: 
   329:   return [...(draws || [])].sort((a, b) => {
   330:     const ya = String(a?.ymd || "");
   331:     const yb = String(b?.ymd || "");
   332:     if (ya !== yb) return ya.localeCompare(yb);
   333: 
   334:     const ha = toNum(a?.close_hour);
   335:     const hb = toNum(b?.close_hour);
   336:     if (ha !== hb) return ha - hb;
   337: 
   338:     const la = normalizeLotteryCodeAny(a);
   339:     const lb = normalizeLotteryCodeAny(b);
   340:     if (la !== lb) return la.localeCompare(lb);
   341: 
   342:     const ia = String(a?.drawId || a?.id || "");
   343:     const ib = String(b?.drawId || b?.id || "");
   344:     return ia.localeCompare(ib);
   345:   });
   346: }

   496: async function fetchPrizesForDraw(drawId, positionsArr, embeddedPrizes) {
   497:   const drawKey = String(drawId || "").trim();
   498:   if (!drawKey) return [];
   499: 
   500:   if (Array.isArray(embeddedPrizes) && embeddedPrizes.length) {
   501:     const normalized = embeddedPrizes.map((p, idx) =>
   502:       normalizePrize(p, p?.prizeId ?? `emb_${idx}`)
   503:     );
   504: 
   505:     const cleaned = normalized.filter(
   506:       (x) => isValidGrupo(x?.grupo) && isValidPosition(x?.position)
   507:     );
   508: 
   509:     if (cleaned.length) {
   510:       const allSorted = sortPrizesByPosition(cleaned);
   511:       return filterPrizesByPositions(allSorted, positionsArr);
   512:     }
   513:   }
   514: 
   515:   const allKey = prizesCacheKeyAll(drawKey);
   516:   const cachedAll = cacheGet(PRIZES_CACHE, allKey);
   517:   if (Array.isArray(cachedAll) && cachedAll.length > 0) {
   518:     return filterPrizesByPositions(cachedAll, positionsArr);
   519:   }

   513:   }
   514: 
   515:   const allKey = prizesCacheKeyAll(drawKey);
   516:   const cachedAll = cacheGet(PRIZES_CACHE, allKey);
   517:   if (Array.isArray(cachedAll) && cachedAll.length > 0) {
   518:     return filterPrizesByPositions(cachedAll, positionsArr);
   519:   }
   520: 
   521:   const prizesCol = collection(db, "draws", drawKey, "prizes");
   522: 
   523:   const { snap, error } = await safeGetDocsSmart(prizesCol, { policy: "server" });
   524:   if (error) throw error;
   525: 
   526:   const allRaw = snap.docs.map((d) => normalizePrize(d.data(), d.id));
   527: 
   528:   // console.log("DEBUG allRaw:", allRaw);
   529: 
   530: const all = allRaw.filter(
   531:   (x) => isValidGrupo(x?.grupo) && isValidPosition(x?.position)
   532: );
   533: 
   534: // console.log("DEBUG allFiltered:", all);
   535: 
   536:   const allSorted = sortPrizesByPosition(all);
   537:   cacheSet(PRIZES_CACHE, allKey, allSorted);
   538: 
   539:   return filterPrizesByPositions(allSorted, positionsArr);
   540: }

   609: async function queryDrawsByField({
   610:   fieldName,
   611:   uf,
   612:   extraWheres = [],
   613:   extraOrderBy = null,
   614:   extraLimit = null,
   615:   policy = DEFAULT_READ_POLICY,
   616: }) {

   623:     ...(extraWheres || []),
   624:   ];

   628: 
   629:   if (extraLimit) parts.push(extraLimit);
   630: 
   631:   const qRef = query(...parts);
   632:   return safeGetDocsSmart(qRef, { policy });

   692:       ufUp === RJ_STATE_CODE ? RJ_LOTTERY_KEY : resolveLotteryKeyForQuery(ufTrim);
   693: 
   694:     const { snap, error } = await queryDrawsByField({
   695:       fieldName: "lottery_key",
   696:       uf: String(lotteryKey).toUpperCase(),
   697:       extraWheres,
   698:       extraOrderBy,
   699:       extraLimit,
   700:       policy,
   701:     });
   702: 
   703:     if (!error && snap?.docs?.length) {
   704:       return { docs: snap.docs, usedField: "lottery_key", error: null };
   705:     }
   706:     if (error) return { docs: [], usedField: "lottery_key", error };
   707:   }
   708: 
   709:   return { docs: [], usedField: "none", error: null };
   710: }

  1073: function collapseFederalDailyCloneDraws(draws) {
  1074:   const arr = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  1075:   const byKey = new Map();
  1076: 
  1077:   for (const d of arr) {
  1078:     const ymd = String(d?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(d?.date) || "").trim();
  1079:     const sig = buildPrizeSignature(d);
  1080: 
  1081:     if (!ymd || !sig) {
  1082:       const fallbackKey = `id::${String(
  1083:         d?.drawId || d?.id || Math.random()
  1084:       )}`;
  1085:       if (!byKey.has(fallbackKey)) byKey.set(fallbackKey, d);
  1086:       continue;
  1087:     }
  1088: 
  1089:     const key = `${ymd}__${sig}`;
  1090:     const prev = byKey.get(key);
  1091: 
  1092:     if (!prev) byKey.set(key, d);
  1093:     else byKey.set(key, pickBetterFederalClone(prev, d));
  1094:   }
  1095: 
  1096:   return sortDrawsLocal(Array.from(byKey.values()));
  1097: }
  1098: 

  1066:   if (ha !== hb) return hb > ha ? b : a;
  1067: 
  1068:   const ia = String(a?.drawId || a?.id || "");
  1069:   const ib = String(b?.drawId || b?.id || "");
  1070:   return ib.localeCompare(ia) > 0 ? b : a;
  1071: }

  1251: function chooseRangeConcurrency(drawCount) {
  1252:   if (!Number.isFinite(drawCount)) return 6;
  1253:   if (drawCount <= 50) return 4;
  1254:   if (drawCount <= 300) return 6;
  1255:   if (drawCount <= 1200) return 8;
  1256:   return 10;
  1257: }

  1268:   if (!Number.isFinite(days) || days <= 0) return "detailed";
  1269:   return "detailed";
  1270: }

  1272: /**
  1273:  * ✅ Fallback barato (SEM prizes): usado quando falta índice composto no RANGE,
  1274:  * ou quando você quer aggregated sem custo de hidratar.
  1275:  */
  1276: async function fetchDrawsDayNoPrizes({ uf, dayYmd }) {
  1277:   const readPolicy = shouldForceFreshServerRead(dayYmd) ? "server" : DEFAULT_READ_POLICY;
  1278: 
  1279:   const { docs, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
  1280:     uf,
  1281:     extraWheres: [where("ymd", "==", dayYmd)],
  1282:     policy: readPolicy,
  1283:   });
  1284:   if (error) throw error;
  1285:   if (!docs || !docs.length) return [];
  1286: 
  1287:   let baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(docs.map(mapDrawDoc));
  1288:   baseAll = baseAll.filter((x) => (x.ymd || normalizeToYMD(x.date)) === dayYmd);
  1289: 
  1290:   const ordered = sortDrawsLocal(baseAll);
  1291: 
  1292:   return ordered.map((d) => {
  1293:     const embedded = normalizeEmbeddedPrizesForAggregated(d?.prizes);
  1294:     return {
  1295:       ...d,
  1296:       prizes: embedded,
  1297:       prizesCount: embedded.length || Number(d?.prizesCount || 0),
  1298:       __mode: "aggregated",
  1299:     };
  1300:   });
  1301: }

  1279:   const { docs, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
  1280:     uf,
  1281:     extraWheres: [where("ymd", "==", dayYmd)],
  1282:     policy: readPolicy,
  1283:   });
  1284:   if (error) throw error;
  1285:   if (!docs || !docs.length) return [];
  1286: 
  1287:   let baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(docs.map(mapDrawDoc));

  1521:     const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
  1522:     const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
  1523:     return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
  1524:   });

  1532: 
  1533: /* =========================
  1534:    ✅ Hidratação de prizes (para UX no "Todos")
  1535: ========================= */
  1536: 
  1537: export async function hydrateKingDrawsWithPrizes({
  1538:   draws,
  1539:   positions = null,
  1540:   concurrency = null,
  1541: }) {
  1542:   const arr = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  1543:   if (!arr.length) return [];
  1544: 
  1545:   const positionsArr = normalizePositions(positions);
  1546: 
  1547:   const conc =
  1548:     Number.isFinite(Number(concurrency)) && Number(concurrency) > 0
  1549:       ? Number(concurrency)
  1550:       : chooseRangeConcurrency(arr.length);
  1551: 

  1520:   const results = await mapWithConcurrency(ordered, conc, async (item) => {
  1521:     const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
  1522:     const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
  1523:     return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
  1524:   });
  1525: 
  1526:   const out = dedupeDrawsLocal(results);
  1527:   if (!bypassCache) {
  1528:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
  1529:     }
  1530:   return out;
  1531: }
  1532: 
  1533: /* =========================
  1534:    ✅ Hidratação de prizes (para UX no "Todos")

  1568:     .map((x) => String(x ?? "").trim().toUpperCase())
